**Question**

On Sunday the temperature was $5$ degrees warmer than on Saturday.

What was the temperature on Sunday?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

What was the temperature on Sunday?

<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Temperature on Sunday} &= \text{Temperature on Saturday} + 5 \\\\
                             &= -1 + 5 \\\\
                             &= 4 \degree  \text{C}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$4 \degree  \text{C}$

</div>

What was the temperature in Paris?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

When the wind blows it feels colder.

The stronger the wind blows, the colder it feels.

Fill in the gaps in the table.

The first row is done for you.

|  Wind Strength  | Temperature out of the wind ($\degree \text{C} $) | How much **colder** it feels in the wind ($\degree \text{C}$)  | Temperature it feels in the wind ($\degree \text{C} $) |
|:---------------:|:--------------------------------------------:|:--------------------------------------------------------:|:-------------------------------------------------:|
| Moderate breeze |                      $5$                     |                    $7$ degrees colder                    |                       $ -2 $                      |
|   Fresh breeze  |                     $-8$                     |                    $11$ degrees colder                   |                      $\ldots$                     |
|  Strong breeze  |                     $-4$                     |                $\ldots \ $ degrees colder                |                       $-20$                       |
|       Gale      |                   $\ldots$                   |                    $23$ degrees colder                   |                       $-45$                       |


</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

| Moderate breeze |                      $5$                     |                    $7$ degrees colder                    |                       $ -2 $                      |
|   Fresh breeze  |                     $-8$                     |                    $11$ degrees colder                   |                      $\ldots$                     |
|  Strong breeze  |                     $-4$                     |                $\ldots \ $ degrees colder                |                       $-20$                       |
|       Gale      |                   $\ldots$                   |                    $23$ degrees colder                   |                       $-45$                       |
<emptyline>
</code></pre>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

How much more than $-3 \degree \text{C}$ is $4 \degree \text{C}$? 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">How much more than $-3 \degree \text{C}$ is $4 \degree \text{C}$? 
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

%empty%
</div>

<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Difference}   &= 4  - (-3) \\\\
                    &= 4 + 3 \\\\
                    &= 7 \degree \text{C}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>

<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

After noon, the temperature fell by $1.5$ degrees each hour. What was the temperature at $18{:}00?$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">After noon, the temperature fell by $1.5$ degrees each hour. What was the temperature at $18{:}00?$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

%empty%
</div>
</div>

%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Temperature fall}                     &= (18{:}00 - 12{:}00) \times 1.5 \\\\
                                            &= 6 \times 1.5 \\\\
                                            &= 9 \degree \text{C} \\\\
\text{Temperature at} \ 18{:}00 \ \text{am} &= 11 - 9 \\\\
                                            &= 2 \degree \text{C}                       
\end{aligned}
$
</div>

**Question**

What was the difference in temperature between midday yesterday and midnight last night in $\degree \text{C}?$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The temperature in Inverness is $12\ \text{degrees}$ lower than the temperature in London. 

The temperature in London is $5\ \text{degrees}$.

What is the temperature in Inverness? 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The temperature in Inverness is $12\ \text{degrees}$ lower than the temperature in London. 
<emptyline>
The temperature in London is $5\ \text{degrees}$.
<emptyline>
What is the temperature in Inverness? 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Temperature in Inverness}    &= \text {Temperature in London} - 12 \\\\
                                    &= 5 - 12 \\\\
                                    &= -7 \degree \text {C}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$-7 \degree \text {C}$

</div>
</div>

**Question**

<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Difference}  &= \text {Temperature in Plymouth} - \text {Temperature in Edinburgh} \\\\
                    &= 5 - (-7) \\\\
                    &= 5 + 7 \\\\
                    &= 12 \degree \text {C}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$12 \degree \text {C}$

</div>

**Question**

Temperatures were taken on one day in each of five cities. The results are shown in the table.

|    City    | Temperature at midnight | Temperature at midday |
|:----------:|:-----------------------:|:---------------------:|
|   Bristol  |    $ 2 \degree \text{C}$       |   $ 8 \degree \text{C}$       |
| Manchester |    $ -2 \degree \text{C}$      |   $ 8 \degree \text{C}$       |
|  Liverpool |    $ -4 \degree \text{C}$      |   $ 7 \degree \text{C}$       |
|   Norwich  |    $ -3 \degree \text{C}$      |   $ -1 \degree \text{C}$      |
|    York    |    $ -7 \degree \text{C}$      |   $ 2 \degree \text{C}$       |


</div>

**Question**

Which city had the greatest rise in temperature between midnight and midday? 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Which city had the greatest rise in temperature between midnight and midday? 
</code></pre>
</div>

</div>

</div>

<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Liverpool

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Temperature Rise}     &= 7 - (-4) \\\\
                            &= 7 + 4 \\\\
                            &= 11 \degree \text{C}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

Liverpool

**Question**

What is the greatest difference in temperature between any two cities at midnight? 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

\end{aligned}
$
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

If it is $-26.5 \degree \text{C}$ in Canada and $34.5 \degree \text{C}$ in Australia, what is the difference in temperature?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">If it is $-26.5 \degree \text{C}$ in Canada and $34.5 \degree \text{C}$ in Australia, what is the difference in temperature?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Difference}   &= 34.5  - (-26.5 ) \\\\
                    &= 34.5 + 26.5   \\\\
                    &= 61 \degree \text{C}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$61 \degree \text{C}$

</div>
</div>

</div>

<div class="workingcodeblock_title">

</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">

<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

From $1800 \ \text{hrs}$ the temperature begins to fall at a steady rate so that by midnight it is $-3 \ \text{degrees}$ and continues to 
fall at this same rate through the rest of the night.

What will the temperature be by $0400 \ \text{hrs}?$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">From $1800 \ \text{hrs}$ the temperature begins to fall at a steady rate so that by midnight it is $-3 \ \text{degrees}$ and continues to 
fall at this same rate through the rest of the night.
<emptyline>
What will the temperature be by $0400 \ \text{hrs}?$
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let the rate of fall of the temperature $= a$

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Temperature at 1800 hrs}  &= 3 + 1.5 \times 6 \\\\
                                &= 3 + 9 \\\\
                                &= 12 \degree \text{C}
\end{aligned}
$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
12 - 6 \times a                 &= -3 \\\\
6a                              &= 12 + 3 \\\\
6a                              &= 15 \\\\
a                               &= \dfrac {15}{6} \\\\
a                               &= \dfrac {5 \times 3}{2 \times 3} \\\\
a                               &= \dfrac {5 \times \cancel{3}}{2 \times \cancel{3}} \\\\
a                               &= \dfrac {5}{2} \\\\
a                               &= 2.5 \degree \text{C} \\\\
\text{Temperature at 0400 hrs}  &= -3 - 4 \times a \\\\
                                &= -3 -4 \times 2.5 \\\\
                                &= -3 -10 \\\\
                                &= -13 \degree \text{C}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$-13 \degree \text{C}$

</div>
</div>

                                &= 3 + 9 \\\\
                                &= 12 \degree \text{C}

<emptyline>
As per the question,
<emptyline>
$
\begin{aligned}
12 - 6 \times a                 &= -3 \\\\
6a                              &= 12 + 3 \\\\
6a                              &= 15 \\\\
a                               &= \dfrac {15}{6} \\\\
a                               &= \dfrac {5 \times 3}{2 \times 3} \\\\
a                               &= \dfrac {5 \times \cancel{3}}{2 \times \cancel{3}} \\\\
a                               &= \dfrac {5}{2} \\\\
a                               &= 2.5 \degree \text{C} \\\\
\text{Temperature at 0400 hrs}  &= -3 - 4 \times a \\\\
                                &= -3 -4 \times 2.5 \\\\
                                &= -3 -10 \\\\
                                &= -13 \degree \text{C}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

A gardener measures the night time temperatures over two evenings and records the results in the table shown below:

|                  | Monday Temperature in degrees Centigrade     | Tuesday Temperature in degree Centigrade     |
|:----------------:|:--------------------------------------------:|:--------------------------------------------:|
| $ 10\ \text{pm}$ |                      $3$                     |                      $2$                     |
| $ 11\ \text{pm}$ |                      $3$                     |                      $1$                     |
| $ 12\ \text{am}$ |                      $2$                     |                      $1$                     |
| $ 1\ \text{am}$  |                      $1$                     |                      $0$                     |
| $ 2\ \text{am}$  |                      $0$                     |                     $-1$                     |
| $ 3\ \text{am}$  |                     $-2$                     |                     $-3$                     |
| $ 4\ \text{am}$  |                     $-1$                     |                     $-1$                     |
| $ 5\ \text{am}$  |                      $0$                     |                      $0$                     |
| $ 6\ \text{am}$  |                      $1$                     |                      $2$                     |
| $ 7\ \text{am}$  |                      $2$                     |                      $4$                     |

On Wednesday the forecast is for all temperatures to drop by $3 \degree \text{C}$ from what they were on Tuesday. 
Bearing in mind his thermometer is broken, what will the actual temperature be on Wednesday at $2 \ \text{am}?$

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

When Greg started to climb Ben Nevis, the temperature at the bottom was $17 \ \text{degrees}$. At the top, it was $-2 \ \text{degrees}$. What was the overall drop in temperature as he climbed up the mountain?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">When Greg started to climb Ben Nevis, the temperature at the bottom was $17 \ \text{degrees}$. At the top, it was $-2 \ \text{degrees}$. What was the overall drop in temperature as he climbed up the mountain?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Difference}   &= 17 - (-2) \\\\
                    &= 17 + 2 \\\\
                    &= 19 \degree
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$19 \degree$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

On January $1^\text{st}$, the temperature in Helsinki was $-4 \degree \text{C}$ and the temperature in Madrid was $12 \degree \text{C}$. The temperature in London was exactly half way between Helsinki and Madrid. What was the temperature in London?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">On January $1^\text{st}$, the temperature in Helsinki was $-4 \degree \text{C}$ and the temperature in Madrid was $12 \degree \text{C}$. The temperature in London was exactly half way between Helsinki and Madrid. What was the temperature in London?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Temperature in London}                        &= \dfrac {-4 + 12 } {2} \\\\
                                                    &= \dfrac {8} {2} \\\\
                                                    &= \dfrac {2 \times 4} {2} \\\\
                                                    &= \dfrac {\cancel{2} \times 4} {\cancel{2}} \\\\
                                                    &= 4 \degree \text{C}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$4 \degree \text{C}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Tips Raw</strong>
</div>

</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="formulacodeblock_title">

</div>

</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="workingcodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Working Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">As per the question,
<emptyline>
$

                                                    &= \dfrac {8} {2} \\\\
                                                    &= \dfrac {2 \times 4} {2} \\\\
                                                    &= \dfrac {\cancel{2} \times 4} {\cancel{2}} \\\\
                                                    &= 4 \degree \text{C}
\end{aligned}
$
</code></pre>
</div>
</div>

<p>reigate-grammar-school--11-plus--maths--2020--sample-paper-7:1:5</p>

<div class="rag">

<div class="topics">

ttemperature
</li>
</ul>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

On January $1^\text{st}$, the temperature in Moscow was -$6 \degree \text{C}$ and the temperature in Rome was $13 \degree \text{C}$. How many degrees warmer was it in Rome than in Moscow?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">On January $1^\text{st}$, the temperature in Moscow was -$6 \degree \text{C}$ and the temperature in Rome was $13 \degree \text{C}$. How many degrees warmer was it in Rome than in Moscow?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
    \text{Degrees warmer}                                       &= \text{Temperature in Rome} - \text{Temperature in Moscow} \\\\
                                                                        &= 13 - (- 6 ) \\\\
                                                                        &= 13 + 6 \\\\
                                                                        &= 19 \degree \text{C}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$19 \degree \text{C}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

In New York it is $23\degree\text{C}$ and in Moscow it is $–5\degree\text{C}$. What is the temperature difference?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

                            &= 23 + 5 \\\\
                            &= 28 \degree \text{C}

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The temperature of the items in a freezer is $-18 \degree \text{C}$. A packet of peas is taken out. After half an hour their temperature has risen by $11 \degree \text{C}$. What is their new temperature?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The temperature of the items in a freezer is $-18 \degree \text{C}$. A packet of peas is taken out. After half an hour their temperature has risen by $11 \degree \text{C}$. What is their new temperature?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{New temperature}      &= -18 + 11 \\\\
                            &= -7 \degree \text{C}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$-7 \degree \text{C}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="tipcodeblock_title">

ttemperature
</li>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

At $5.00 \ \text{am}$ the temperature is $-3 \degree \text{C}$. At $10{:}30 \ \text{am}$ the temperature has risen steadily by $2 \degree \text{C per hour}$. What is the temperature at $10{:}30  \ \text{am} \text{?}$ 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">At $5.00 \ \text{am}$ the temperature is $-3 \degree \text{C}$. At $10{:}30 \ \text{am}$ the temperature has risen steadily by $2 \degree \text{C per hour}$. What is the temperature at $10{:}30  \ \text{am} \text{?}$ 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Temperature rise}                     &= (10{:}30 \ \text{am} - 5{:}00 \ \text{am}) \times 2 \\\\
                                            &= 5 \dfrac{1} {2} \ \text{hours} \times 2 \\\\ 
                                            &= \dfrac{11 \times 2} {2} \\\\
                                            &= \dfrac{11 \times \cancel 2} { \cancel 2} \\\\
                                            &= 11 \degree \text{C} \\\\
\text{Temperature at} \ 10{:}30 \ \text{am} &= -3 + 11 \\\\
                                            &= 8 \degree \text{C}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$8 \degree \text {C}$

</div>
</div>

                                            &= \dfrac{11 \times 2} {2} \\\\

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The temperature recorded on one day in $5$ different cities in the world are shown below.

$-12 \degree \text{C} \quad 8 \degree \text{C} \quad 19 \degree \text{C} \quad {-}4 \degree \text{C} \quad 23 \degree \text{C}$


What is the difference in temperature between the hottest and the coldest
cities? 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The temperature recorded on one day in $5$ different cities in the world are shown below.
<emptyline>
$-12 \degree \text{C} \quad 8 \degree \text{C} \quad 19 \degree \text{C} \quad {-}4 \degree \text{C} \quad 23 \degree \text{C}$
<emptyline>

What is the difference in temperature between the hottest and the coldest
cities? 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Difference}   &= 23 - (- 12) \\\\
                     &= 23 + 12 \\\\
                     &= 35 \degree \text{C}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$35\degree \text{C}$

</div>
</div>

than the average for January.

**Question**

In the evening, the temperature in Liverpool was $2 \degree \text{C}$ colder than in the morning.

Write down the temperature in Liverpool in the evening,

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>

<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">In the evening, the temperature in Liverpool was $2 \degree \text{C}$ colder than in the morning.
<emptyline>
Write down the temperature in Liverpool in the evening,
</code></pre>
</div>

<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Temperature in the evening}  &= \text {Temperature in the morning} - 2 \\\\
                                    &= -1 - 2 \\\\
                                    &= -3 \degree \text {C}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

How many degrees warmer was it inside than outside the window?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">How many degrees warmer was it inside than outside the window?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Difference} &= 21 - (-3) \\\\
                   &= 21 + 3 \\\\
                   &= 24 \degree \text {C}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>

<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Over the Christmas holidays this year the maximum temperature at a teacher's house was $7.20$ degrees Celsius. 

The temperature has now **fallen** by $3.15$ degrees, what is the temperature?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Over the Christmas holidays this year the maximum temperature at a teacher's house was $7.20$ degrees Celsius. 
<emptyline>

<li class="questionContainer">
<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subsubquestion">
<div class="question subsubquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What temperature is $20 \degree \text{C}$ in $\degree \text{F}$?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What temperature is $20 \degree \text{C}$ in $\degree \text{F}$?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Temperature}  &= 20 \degree \text{C} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac{9} {5} \times 20 + 32 \ \degree \text{F} \\\\
                    &= \dfrac{9 \times 20} {5} + 32 \\\\
                    &= \dfrac{9 \times 5 \times 4} {5} + 32 \\\\
                    &= \dfrac{9 \times \cancel{5} \times 4} {\cancel{5}} + 32 \\\\
                    &= 9 \times 4 + 32 \\\\
                    &= 36 + 32 \\\\
                    &= 68 \degree \text{F}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$68  \degree \text{F}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">
<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question subsubquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

What temperature is $59 \degree \text{F}$ in $\degree \text{C}$?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">What temperature is $59 \degree \text{F}$ in $\degree \text{C}$?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let temperature in $\degree \text{C} = a$

As per the equation,

$
\begin{aligned}
59                      &= \dfrac{9} {5} \times a + 32 \\\\
\dfrac{9} {5} \times a  &= 59 - 32 \\\\
\dfrac{9 \times a} {5}  &= 27 \\\\
a                       &= \dfrac{27 \times 5} {9} \\\\
a                       &= \dfrac{9 \times 3 \times 5} {9} \\\\
a                       &= \dfrac{\cancel{9} \times 3 \times 5} {\cancel{9}} \\\\
a                       &= 3 \times 5 \\\\
a                       &= 15 \degree \text{C}
\end{aligned}
$

Temperature in $\degree \text{C} = 15 \degree \text{C}$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$15  \degree \text{C}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="tipcodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

On Monday it was was $-12 \degree \text{C}$. The temperature rose by $1 \degree \text{C}$ each day for the next week. What was the temperature on Friday?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">On Monday it was was $-12 \degree \text{C}$. The temperature rose by $1 \degree \text{C}$ each day for the next week. What was the temperature on Friday?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Temperature on Friday} &= -12 + 1 + 1 + 1 + 1 \\\\
                             &= -12 + 4 \\\\
                             &= -8 \degree \text{C}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$-8 \degree \text{C}$

</div>
</div>

</div>

                             &= -8 \degree \text{C}

</code></pre>

**Question**

At the same time, the tempratures in Val Thorens is $7$ degrees colder.

What is the temprature in Val Thorens?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<emptyline>

$

<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">On the same day, the temperature in Rome was $12 \degree \text{C}$.
<emptyline>
How many degrees hotter was it in Rome than in London?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Difference in temperature}  &= \text {Rome Temperature - London Temperature} \\\\
                                   &= 12  - (-2 ) \\\\
                                   &= 12 + 2 \\\\
                                   &= 14 \degree \text {C}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$14 \degree \text {C}$

</div>
</div>

**Question**

The table below shows the temperatures in three towns on Monday.

|    town   | temperature, in $\degree \text{C}$ |
|:---------:|:--------------------------------:|
|  Sumville |                $8$               |
| Addington |               $-11$              |
| Totalford |               $-6$               |

![papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2014/group-1/s01-q13-i00.png](/assets/papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2014/group-1/s01-q13-i00.png "papers/the-london-independent-girls-schools-consortium/2014/group-1/s01-q13-i00.png")

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

How many degrees warmer was it in Sumville than in Addington?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">How many degrees warmer was it in Sumville than in Addington?
</code></pre>

<div class="workings">

                        &= 8 + 11 \\\\
                        &= 19 \degree \text{C}
\end{aligned}
$

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Field crickets chirp according to the temperature of their surroundings.

You can use the following rule to work out the temperature:

$\text{temperature in} \degree \text{C} = \text{(number of chirps per minute} - 40 \text{)} \div 7 + 10$

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

Freddie, the field cricket, chirps $61 \ \text{times per minute}$.

Work out the temperature of Freddie's surroundings.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">Freddie, the field cricket, chirps $61 \ \text{times per minute}$.
<emptyline>
Work out the temperature of Freddie's surroundings.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Freddie's surroundings temperature}   &= (61 - 40) \div 7 + 10 \\\\
                                            &= 21 \div 7 + 10 \\\\
                                            &= 3 + 10 \\\\
                                            &= 13 \degree \text{C}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$13 \degree \text{C}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question_envelope rag_not_found subquestion">
<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The temperature of Flossie's surroundings is $18 \degree \text{C}$.

Work out how many times Flossie chirps in $1 \ \text{minute}$.

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The temperature of Flossie's surroundings is $18 \degree \text{C}$.
<emptyline>
Work out how many times Flossie chirps in $1 \ \text{minute}$.
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

Let Flossie chirps (in $1 \ \text{minute}$) $= a$

As per the question,

$
\begin{aligned}
18                          &= (a - 40) \div 7 + 10 \\\\
18                          &= \dfrac {a - 40} {7} + 10 \\\\
\dfrac {a - 40} {7} + 10    &= 18 \\\\
\dfrac {a - 40} {7}         &= 18 - 10 \\\\
\dfrac {a - 40} {7}         &= 8 \\\\
a                           &= 8 \times 7 + 40 \\\\
a                           &= 56 + 40 \\\\
a                           &= 96
\end{aligned}
$

Flossie chirps (in $1 \ \text{minute}$) $= 96$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$96$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question subquestion" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

On Wednesday the temperature was $5 \degree \text{C}$.

How many degrees warmer was it on Wednesday than on Monday?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">On Wednesday the temperature was $5 \degree \text{C}$.
<emptyline>
How many degrees warmer was it on Wednesday than on Monday?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Degrees warmer}               &= 5 - (-4) \\\\
                                    &= 5 + 4 \\\\
                                    &= 9 \degree \text{C}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$9 \degree \text{C}$

</div>
</div>
<div class="workingscodeblock">

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

In Moscow, the temperature is $-11 \degree \text{C}$ and in Paris the temperature is $14 \ \text{degrees}$ warmer.


What is the temperature in Paris?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">In Moscow, the temperature is $-11 \degree \text{C}$ and in Paris the temperature is $14 \ \text{degrees}$ warmer.
<emptyline>

What is the temperature in Paris?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Temperature in Paris}  &= \text {Temperature in Moscow} + 14 \\\\
                              &= -11 + 14 \\\\
                              &= 3 \degree \text {C}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$3 \degree \text {C}$

</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The temperature inside Nanook's igloo is $9 \degree \text{C}$ and the temperature outside is $-12 \degree \text{C}$.

How many degrees warmer is it inside than outside? 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The temperature inside Nanook's igloo is $9 \degree \text{C}$ and the temperature outside is $-12 \degree \text{C}$.
<emptyline>
How many degrees warmer is it inside than outside? 
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Formulas**

<div class="formula">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text {Difference}  &= 9 - (- 12)\\\\
                    &= 9 + 12 \\\\
                    &= 21 \degree \text {C}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$21 \degree \text {C}$

</div>
</div>

**Question**

The table below shows the highest and lowest temperatures in $\degree\text{C}$ recorded in six cities around the world on one particular day 
last year.

{/*
**City**            ,**Highest temperature** $(\degree \text{C})$   ,**Lowest temperature** $(\degree \text{C})$
Boston              ,$30$                                           ,$8$
Munich              ,$19$                                           ,$-6$
Los Angeles         ,$23$                                           ,$11$
Cairo               ,$32$                                           ,$19$
St Petersburg       ,$9$                                            ,$-8$
Budapest            ,$10$                                           ,$-9$
*/}

|    **City**       | **Highest temperature** $(\degree \text{C})$     | **Lowest temperature** $(\degree \text{C})$     |
|:-------------:    |:--------------------------------------------:    |:-------------------------------------------:    |

|  Los Angeles      |                     $23$                         |                     $11$                        |
|     Cairo         |                     $32$                         |                     $19$                        |

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

In Moscow at noon it is $4 \degree \text{C}$. By midnight the temperature has dropped by $10 \degree \text{C}$. What is the temperature at midnight? 

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

</div>

</code></pre>

                                &= -6 \degree \text {C}
\end{aligned}
$

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

On Christmas day the temperature in Lisbon was $-5 \degree \text{C}$. On New Year's day the temperature was $3$ $\text{degrees}$ lower. What was the temperature on New Year's day?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">

</code></pre>

$

\text {Temperature on New Year's day}    &= -5 - 3 \\\\
                                         &= -8 \degree \text {C}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>

<div class="question" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Question**

The temperature at midday in Moscow is $-3\degree\,\text{C}$. Between midday and midnight the temperature in Moscow rises by $5\degree\,\text{C}$ and then falls by $11\degree\,\text{C}$. 

What is the temperature at midnight in Moscow?

</div>
<div class="questioncodeblock" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">
<div class="questioncodeblock_title">
<strong style="display: block; margin-bottom: 0.5rem;">Question Raw</strong>
</div>
<pre class="language-latex"><code class="language-latex">The temperature at midday in Moscow is $-3\degree\,\text{C}$. Between midday and midnight the temperature in Moscow rises by $5\degree\,\text{C}$ and then falls by $11\degree\,\text{C}$. 
<emptyline>
What is the temperature at midnight in Moscow?
</code></pre>
</div>
<div class="workings">
<div class="tips placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Tips**

<div class="tip">
%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="formulas placeholder" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

%empty%
</div>
</div>
<div class="working" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Working**

$
\begin{aligned}
\text{Temperature at midnight}  &= -3 + 5 - 11 \\\\
                                &= 2 - 11 \\\\
                                &= -9 \degree \text{C}
\end{aligned}
$
</div>
</div>
<div class="answers">
<div class="answer" style="margin: 1rem 0; padding: 0.85rem 1rem; border: 1px solid #9ea7b3; border-radius: 6px;">

**Answer**

$-9 \degree \text{C}$

</div>
</div>
